C) NEXT DECISION
============================================================
If /api/activity/start requires activityTypeId/template, we will either:
1) use the correct required fields for /api/activity/start; or
2) create via /api/activity/record and transition through /api/activity/events/[id] if PATCH/PUT supports it; or
3) add a controlled debug setup route only for creating a non-completed event, while keeping /api/activity/complete as the production route under test.
